# ENGINE — Pascal-style first draft

Scope: main_0000, res_00BA, res_03F1, res_042B, res_0464, res_0709, res_0802, res_0899, res_08AE. 132/132 exported routines have sections, plus five newly found sound interrupt/helper entries absent from the export. This is an evidence-preserving first draft for verification, not a decode-gate approval or executable engine.

## Evidence and notation

Listing snapshot: git `8d268a1e285ccdf55099da1f53a28a64259272d0`. Listings were being regenerated by another lane during this task, so this draft reads the committed `.lst`/`.c` snapshot rather than partially written files. Original resident bytes are read from START.unpacked.exe and disassembled with Capstone when checking hardware/segment behavior. Read FOREMAN.md, LAYOUT.md, listings/INDEX.md and the runtime ledger (now Opus verified). ECL_VM.md was initially absent, then became available and was read for interpreter entry points, memory regions and ENGINE call boundaries. This draft does not assign ECL opcodes.

H/M/L in routine titles concerns the inferred role/name. Pascal-like bodies preserve the decompiler control flow and numeric expressions; unproven record layouts stay byte/pointer accesses, and register/flag artifacts stay explicit unknowns. The original FUN identifiers remain in calls so every cross-reference resolves back to an input listing.

- Statement comments without a qualifier identify a matched call/port/store/return instruction or a manually reviewed instruction anchor. A comment containing `region anchor, exact origin unknown` identifies only the containing code region; it is NOT an exact per-statement location. Those mappings remain L and must be refined before a verified translation.
- Params are the decompiler ABI view (nearest return address first), not a recovered original Pascal signature. Pascal source formal order is reversed; the BP access inventory in each header is the authoritative byte-offset evidence. Locals `local_N` refer to Ghidra entry-SP offsets: in a conventional PUSH BP/MOV BP,SP frame, BP offset = 2-N. Nested helpers 0709:06A7/0714 use a parent BP frame.
- `Ptr<T>(address)^`, `MemByte/Word/Far`, `BPByte/Word/Far`, `BPAddr`, `FarPtr(segment,offset)`, `MakeWord/Long`, `ExtractBytes`, `PackedSet` and `Seq` are pseudocode primitives, not game functions. `Seq` evaluates left-to-right and returns the last expression; `and then`/`or else` short-circuit. `IfThenElseLazy` evaluates only the chosen arm.
- `PString(address)` is a length-prefixed Pascal string lvalue. Runtime copy/append becomes assignment/concat; `UNK_hidden_string_result` denotes an output pointer retained on the caller stack which the decompiler failed to reconstruct. Do not equate different unknown hidden destinations across calls. Exact file/string parameter groups remain unresolved where marked.
- `UNK_extraout_*`, `UNK_in_*` and `UNK_unaff_*` are unresolved register/segment values, not globals. Decompiled Boolean temporaries after CompareStrFlags or set membership may represent FLAGS rather than actual variables; those conditions are unverified. Manual Main/ReadGameKey bodies correct the flag artifacts from the branch instructions.
- All `g_XXXX` are offsets in DS=0C7C. DAT_10BF_YYYY aliases normalize to DS:(YYYY+4430h). 08AE sound helpers explicitly switch DS to CS; their private variables are `cs08AE_XXXX`, not game DS globals. Ghidra string-shaped symbols used as integers are replaced by their numeric address, never treated as actual text without a Pascal-string load.

Local correction to RT_LEDGER: **0AF8:1593 is FilePos**, despite the map label FileSize. 15DA obtains current position with AX=4201, saves it, obtains end with4202, restores current with4200; it returns current in DX:AX and end in BX:CX. 1593 returns DX:AX divided by record size, whereas 15AA first moves CX/BX to AX/DX and therefore returns FileSize. 0802:0860 uses1593 to stop the directory scan at dataStart. This draft renders it FilePos; shared runtime files are unchanged. Evidence: raw 0AF8:15E8..1612 and 15AA..15BF, plus 0802:0860..0881. Confidence H.

ENGINE area correction: 00BA is shared item/character/combat/UI support, not just file loading; 08AE is sound/timer support with PIT/PSG I/O and a hidden INT08 handler, not merely video or a sine table.

Cross-lane checks at handoff: the current regenerated functions.tsv still lists exactly these132 ENGINE routines; none are missing. The near-branch repair in LAYOUT affects ov03/ov19/ov22, so no ENGINE entry addresses changed. GLOBALS.md and STRINGS.md were also read after they landed. All referenced static literals for these nine units in strings.tsv are included below; apparent sound-code strings at08AE:13FF onward are instruction-byte noise, not screens.

ECL integration (ECL_VM.md §§1/3): main calls ov03:32F8 = ecl_GameLoop through stub_ov03_e1. ECL loading lives in ov07:0341 and calls ENGINE LoadDaxRecord0802:0716. DS:49D2/49D6/49DA point to VM word regions0/1/2; DS:49DE points to byte code region3. DS:5D92 is selected character; DS:6AAD/6AAE are party coordinates and6AAF facing. ENGINE messages, picture drawing and data-set switching are shared services; opcode handlers are outside this area's ownership. These boundaries are cross-references to that lane's evidence, not duplicated opcode decode.
